Notes: Filmed in 4K from balcony. WIder shots during ensemble numbers and spelling segments to see the speller, Rona, and Panch; closer zooms for individual focus. Includes curtain call. The 25th Annual Putnam County Spelling Bee

Off-Broadway Revival

2025

MTTZ

Video

Cast: Lilli Cooper (Rona Lisa Peretti), Emily N. Rudolph (u/s Olive Ostrovsky), Kevin McHale (William Barfée), Autumn Best (Logainne Schwartzandgrubenierre), Matt Manuel (Mitch Mahoney), Justin Cooley (Leaf Coneybear), Leana Rae Concepcion (Marcy Park), Philippe Arroyo (Chip Tolentino), Jason Kravits (Douglas Panch)
Notes: Excellent capture of this wonderful revival! Shot in a great mix of wides, mediums, and close-ups, with a focus on wides in earlier parts of the show to get all the acting choices. Some increased wandering, shaking, and re-adjusting throughout. Audio is patched in from an external source. Some disruptive audience noises. A few brief dropouts and obstructions from latecomers, as well as obstruction on the bottom and left side of the screen that is worked around well and never obstructs significant action. Overall an excellent capture, and about as good as you'll get in that space. This video should not be listed with a month.
Format: MP4 (7.22 GB)

The 25th Annual Putnam County Spelling Bee

Off-Broadway Revival

2025-12

The Riddle

Video

Cast: Lilli Cooper (Rona Lisa Peretti), Jasmine Rogers (Olive Ostrovsky), Kevin McHale (William Barfée), Autumn Best (Logainne Schwartzandgrubenierre), Matt Manuel (Mitch Mahoney), Justin Cooley (Leaf Coneybear), Leana Rae Concepcion (Marcy Park), Philippe Arroyo (Chip Tolentino), Jason Kravits (Douglas Panch), Michael Urie (g/p Guest Speller)
Notes: Good 4k capture of this fun show! Shot in a mix of mediums, wides, and closeups, with some more wide shots to emphasize the ensemble nature of the piece. Starts slightly after Lilli's entrance, with the first thirty seconds of video blacked out, and another blackout about 10 minutes in, when the audience was asked to stand up. Shot over the mezzanine ledge to the best of my ability, but it appears in most wide shots. Some shakiness from readjusting the frame every so often to ensure the ledge's presence was minimal, and actor's feet can be blocked off by it, but most mediums and closeups are not affected. I was sitting next to perhaps the loudest laugher in the entire world, apologies about that. Includes bows.
Format: MP4 (8.27 GB)

video

Assassins

Broadway

2004-04-10

Unknown

video

Cast: Michael Cerveris (John Wilkes Booth), Denis O'Hare (Charles Guiteau), James Barbour (Leon Czolgosz), Mario Cantone (Samuel Byck), Jeffrey Kuhn (Giuseppe Zangara), Mary Catherine Garrison (Lynette "Squeaky" Fromme), Neil Patrick Harris (Lee Harvey Oswald/Balladeer), Becky Ann Baker (Sara Jane Moore), Marc Kudisch (The Proprietor), Alexander Gemignani (John Hinckley Jr.), Anne L. Nathan (Emma Goldman)
Notes: After rummaging through old trading sites and checking different videos it seems that April 10 (the version with no generation loss) is the correct date and is from the master copy. This master's videos are notorious for having playback glitches and this video is an example of that. While there's no real fix for the glitchy playback, it has been recommend on sites to convert the disc to an MKV file or play it through certain apps, specifically the Mac DVD player app.
There are 2 full length videos from this production that circulate with various dates and copies. This one is most commonly listed as April 4th or 10th, but has also been listed as April 20th, 24th, and has even been mixed up with the May 29th video. Check your copy against the screenshots to see which one you have.
Two versions of this particular recording circulate: one with no playback glitches but lots of generation loss (April, 2004 or April 4 - 3.98 VOB and smalls) and one with glitchy playback but no generation loss (April 10, 2004, Master copy screenshots below - 3.51 VOB and smalls)
Format: VOB + smalls (3.51 GB)

Cabaret

Fifth West End Revival

2022-01

Jagweed

video

Cast: Eddie Redmayne (Emcee), Jessie Buckley (Sally Bowles), Omari Douglas (Clifford Bradshaw), Liza Sadovy (Fräulein Schneider), Elliot Levey (Herr Schultz), Stewart Clarke (Ernst Ludwig), Anna-Jane Casey (Fräulein Kost/Fritzie), Sally Frith (Frenchie), Andre Refig (Max), Daniel Perry (Victor), Theo Maddix (Lulu), Christopher Tendai (Bobby), Sophie Maria Wojna (Rosie), B Terry (Helga), Matthew Gent (Hans), Lillie-Pearl Wildman (Texas)
Notes: This is the only publicly released video recording of this acclaimed, sold out production with the original West End revival cast. This was my first time recording and as a result, mistakes were made. Foolishly, I set the exposure to automatic, so the recording suffered from spotlight washout (I haven't made this mistake since). The washout has mostly been fixed in editing but it's impossible to fully correct. The recording has more zooms than I would like, to prevent further washout. Fortunately, this production is minimalistic and performance-focused so it doesn't hinder the overall experience of the show. The recording is mainly filmed in close-ups and mediums. A head is sometimes visible in the wide shots of the musical numbers. Recording includes the curtain call and marquee.
Format: MP4 (9.76 GB)

Broadway

2004-01-09

StageStruckEurope

video

Cast: Idina Menzel (Elphaba), Kristin Chenoweth (Glinda), Taye Diggs (t/r Fiyero), Joel Grey (The Wizard), Carole Shelley (Madame Morrible), Michelle Federer (Nessarose), Christopher Fitzgerald (Boq), William Youmans (Doctor Dillamond)
Notes: Last three caps are from the original DV capture shot in PAL. It's a 21 year old video. If it were a child, I'd be buying it a drink now, so... the quality was great for its time, and it's an improvement over what's floating around.
This was the first full length video I'd filmed, so there was plenty of upper body cramping, which is the reason for the camera shaking, but I thin k it was the only video that came out with Idina/Taye, so we take what we can :-)
Restored to the original PAL, though not without a hit from the gen loss incurred along the way. Audio has been filtered to remove the camera noise and excess hiss from the theatre. Image is on the shaky side, and the camera has a hard time locking into focus, combined with being out of sync with the US lights leading to a constant flicker. A pity because its a strong performance by the original cast during the show's ascent.
Sometimes mislabeled as September 1, 2004; Taye was only in the show from December 22 2003 - January 18 2004 while Norbert Leo Butz was out due to a back injury. The audio circulating as January 8, 2004 is ripped from this video, but the audio rip is better quality than the audio in the video.
As of 2025, the master has confirmed that they have the original unconverted recording, which is better quality than the circulating version(s).
Format: VOB + smalls (4.16 GB)

Multiple copies are circulating. The best is the 7.8 GB file, which has higher video resolution, working menus, and the playbill. The 7.63 GB file has the same video quality but no playbill, skips 1 minute in late Act 1 (A Sentimental Man (Reprise)), skips the first 1.5 minutes of Act 2, and the smalls may glitch such that the last section of Act 1 doesn't play at all. The 4.07 GB file has worse video quality but does have a working menu, and includes the playbill and various extras (TV spots, Macy's special, Seth's Broadway Chatterbox).
Format: VOB + smalls (7.63 GB)
